## `AGO-4321-MGT-R4-MGT-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `MGT`
- **Role:** RCD-Trip-Level Safety Officer
- **Scenario:** Shift Handover, Material Staging Verification & Bottleneck Takt-Time Balancing SOP for Building Busway Trunking & Low/High Voltage Switchgear Busbar Electrician

All work orders must be signed and dated. No open LOTO permits without a valid handover signature.

## `AGO-4321-MGT-R4-MGT-SC-02`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `MGT`
- **Role:** RCD-Trip-Level Safety Officer
- **Scenario:** 4M1E Engineering Change (ECN) Containment Approval & 8D/CAPA Root Cause Rectification SOP for Construction Site Temporary Power TN-S & RCD Electric Shock Safety Incidents

## `AGO-4321-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** GIS-Switchgear Specialist
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Building Busway Trunking & Low/High Voltage Switchgear Busbar Electrician
- **Intro:** GIS-Switchgear Specialist — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Building Busway Trunking & Low/High Voltage Switchgear Busbar Electrician — Verify Lockout/Tagout (LOTO) and Zero-Energy State: Voltage reading must be 0 V AC on all phases and ground. Residual voltage must be below 50 V AC or 70 V DC.
- **Key tolerance:** Voltage reading must be 0 V AC on all phases and ground. Residual voltage must be below 50 V AC or 70 V DC.
- **Standards:** `IEC 62271-200:2021`, `IEC 61439-1:2020`, `IEC 60364-6:2016`, `ANSI/NETA ATS-2021 Standard for Acceptance Testing Specification`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/AGO-4321-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/AGO-4321-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/AGO-4321-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/AGO-4321-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/AGO-4321-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/AGO-4321-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/AGO-4321-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/AGO-4321-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/AGO-4321-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/AGO-4321-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/AGO-4321-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01.html)

## `AGO-4321-OPS-R2-OPS-SC-02`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Busway-Trunking Mechanic
- **Scenario:** In-situ Removal, Reassembly & Dynamic Tolerance Reset SOP for Emergency Diesel Generator ATS Switch & Central UPS Wear Components
- **Intro:** Busway-Trunking Mechanic — In-situ Removal, Reassembly & Dynamic Tolerance Reset SOP for Emergency Diesel Generator ATS Switch & Central UPS Wear Components — Lockout/Tagout and Zero-Energy Verification: Capacitor residual voltage must be ≤ 5 V DC after discharge. Voltage tester must be rated CAT IV 600 V AC/DC.
- **Key tolerance:** Capacitor residual voltage must be ≤ 5 V DC after discharge. Voltage tester must be rated CAT IV 600 V AC/DC.
- **Standards:** `IEC 61439-1:2020`, `IEC 60947-6-1:2021`, `IEC 62040-3:2021`, `No. 77`

- **Role:** RCD-Trip-Level Safety Officer
- **Scenario:** 4M1E Engineering Change (ECN) Containment Approval & 8D/CAPA Root Cause Rectification SOP for Construction Site Temporary Power TN-S & RCD Electric Shock Safety Incidents
